## What is still open / ambiguous for Claude Code

1. **Full rebuild vs preservation — RESOLVED Nov 8, 2026.** New build from scratch is DECIDED. The Oct 30 preservation rule is superseded for this build. BUILD HOLD still stands: do not build anything until Mahendra says so.
2. **Do not hand the prototype HTML to Claude Code as code.** The Oct 30 lock says the Muse HTML is a UI prototype reference ONLY, not production code. Hand over THIS catalog + the design intent + the locked rules; Claude Code rebuilds from the spec.
3. **Commission form undecided:** % per redeemed order vs flat per order vs monthly per restaurant; per-restaurant vs per-Drop; whether creator share comes out of that commission or is separate. Prototype uses % per redeemed order, 12% SAMPLE ONLY — no real rate agreed.
4. **Creator model not fully locked:** one permanent creator link (parent) with auto-generated per-Drop links is a proposal only; manual parent/child management was judged "too complex to scale". Creator Drops bundle-only / $25+ is NOT separately locked. Dallas only for now.
5. **Drops per restaurant — RESOLVED Nov 8, 2026.** Smart menu is 3 to 4 Drops; 3 is the sweet spot, 4 is the max, never a filler Drop. Supersedes the old 2–3 target.
6. **"First Drop goes live moves a restaurant to Doing Business" is a stated rule in the prototype, not a verified transition.** No live Drop exists in the prototype. Claude Code must implement it as an explicit, confirmed transition (operator confirms the move), not a silent auto-move — consistent with the no-auto-advance rule.
7. **Native app — RESOLVED Nov 8, 2026 (locked as UC-243).** No native app yet: mobile-first web Studio, installable via Add to Home Screen. Build a native app only if multiple daily operators, reliable push alerts, or offline QR scanning become required.
8. **Business-model conflict (from the Claude Code briefing, unresolved):** "$99–199/month subscription base" vs "$0 unless an order gets picked up" performance promise are different commercial offers. Do not pitch restaurants until Mahendra confirms the pilot's actual terms. This now also blocks UC-246 settlement terms (payout schedule and hold) — see open item 14.
9. **Agent system is DESIGN ONLY (endorsed Nov 2).** One orchestrator + specialists, human-gated, disagreements block publishing. It is NOT a build authorization; the Nov 7 Gate 1 go-ahead covers staging generally, not the agent system. First-agent bottleneck (operator minutes vs creator distribution vs restaurant onboarding) is still unanswered.
10. **All prototype figures are sample data.** Every count, dollar amount, customer, and pipeline lead in the prototype is illustrative. Claude Code must build against real data models, not copy sample values (e.g. do NOT treat 12% commission, $97.00 gross, or the 7 sample customers as real).
11. **Desktop top-tab overflow (minor, prototype):** at 1440px the top-tab row clips "Outcomes" and "190 Use Cases" (reachable by horizontal scroll). Mobile layout passed at 390px with 0px overflow. The real build must not clip nav at desktop widths.
12. **Prototype "Preview" items (9) need server-side enforcement in the real build:** UC-085 (play actual video), UC-090 (immutable media version), UC-105 (prevent overselling), UC-151/152 (auth/roles), UC-157/158 (deletion eligibility / state transitions server-side), UC-186 (bulk cancel), UC-189 (idempotent refunds). "Managed here" in the prototype means a working control in a static mock, NOT production enforcement.
13. **QR scaling — specified in V2.1 (UC-232), implementation detail still to be proven in staging.** The rule is atomic redemption + atomic inventory decrement in Supabase, QR generated on demand. Claude Code must prove it with a simultaneous-purchase / simultaneous-scan test in staging before prod.
14. **Stripe Connect DECIDED as the payout rail (Nov 8, 2026) — what remains open on top of Connect:** (a) payout schedule/timing for creators and restaurants (weekly proposed — confirm) and minimum creator payout; (b) restaurant payout hold threshold; (c) attribution rule — RESOLVED Nov 8, 2026 (V2.7): last-tagged-link-wins, LOCKED (Mahendra). UC-248, UC-239, UC-313 updated; visit-opener-wins is REJECTED (recorded in §30). No longer open; (d) messaging frequency cap (UC-250: 1 marketing message per customer per week unless they claimed — confirm); (e) creator self-service form (UC-252: portal vs link + WhatsApp at first scale); (f) tax handling details (UC-253: confirm with preparer that Connect handles 1099/W-9; do not hard-code tax law into the catalog without advice); (g) the business-model conflict in open item 8, which blocks settlement terms. Connect solves onboarding, bank accounts, split payouts, and tax-form / 1099 reporting. It does NOT solve schedule, holds, attribution, or support — those are DealsPro rules to decide.
15. **Cancel policy — PROPOSED, not agreed (Nov 8, 2026, §25 UC-254–UC-269 + UC-479–UC-488).** Mahendra has not yet agreed the cancel policy. The whole of §25 — the Ready-based cutoff, refund timing, whole-Drop cancel rules, reliability strikes, no-show rule, refund money rules, and the Ready mechanism — is a proposal from the Nov 8 conversation. **V2.10 model (Nov 8, 2026, Mahendra: "this is very critical"):** the customer deals with the restaurant directly after placing the order; the restaurant's number is sent to the customer to call for any changes; the restaurant decides and cancels in-system (pickup-link Cancel tap or reply CANCEL [code]); DealsPro syncs back (QR invalidated, inventory restored, refund automatic). The operator-decides / customer-self-serve-cancel model from V2.3 is SUPERSEDED — see the superseded notes on UC-254–UC-257, UC-261, UC-263, UC-267. Do not build §25 as locked policy, and do not present it to restaurants or customers as agreed terms, until he taps "agree" on this model. If he changes the cutoff (e.g. a different point than Ready) or the decision-maker, §25 must be revised before build. The Ready mechanism itself (private pickup link, reply READY, Studio Ready button) is likewise proposed, not locked.
16. **Studio catalog vs file catalog — V2.8 state (Nov 8, 2026): CORRECTED figures; still not closed.** The mismatch fix verified the Studio catalog at **442 cases rendered, not 439** as the Studio headline claimed (COV array counted section by section, twice) — the V2.7 wording of this item (439, 10-case difference) was therefore wrong on both numbers and is corrected here. The gap at V2.7 was **13 cases (442 Studio − 429 file)**. V2.8 adds the 5 genuinely new mismatch cases (UC-430–UC-434) and the 30 monitoring cases (UC-435–UC-464), taking the file to 464 — so the file/Studio comparison is no longer a single-number gap and must not be stated as one: the Studio's §§1–35 remainder (8 cases at V2.7, unverified verbatim — the file would be 434 after the 5 new cases, leaving an 8-case remainder in §§1–35) is still open, the Studio headline still says 439 and must be corrected to 442, and the Studio's stale stored section-start labels (e.g. §4 "UC-031" vs rendered UC-033–044, §18 "UC-176" vs UC-191–205, §41 "UC-428" vs UC-431–442) are still Studio repairs, not file repairs. THIS FILE remains the single source of truth. Do not close this item until the §§1–35 remainder is extracted case-by-case from the Studio and either mapped or added, and the Studio headline/labels are fixed. Numbering differs by design: the Studio renumbers sequentially from UC-001, while this file keeps V1.0-derived IDs plus appended blocks; compare by section and content using the Studio-source mapping on every V2.5/V2.7 case, never ID-to-ID. The Studio artifact was NOT edited in this reconciliation. Remaining Studio-side work (for a future Studio rebuild driven from this file): fix the Studio's stale stored section-start labels for §1–§18, and correct its "the agreed policy" label on §33 to PROPOSED. The Studio's "Scale & payouts — today's watch" section on Today was reported empty in an earlier audit capture and later reported fixed by the Studio build (a variable-shadowing bug) — verify on the live Studio before relying on it.

17. **Claim → restaurant confirm → Ready flow — PROPOSED, not agreed (Nov 8, 2026, §26 UC-270–UC-282).** Mahendra has not yet agreed this flow. The whole of §26 — immediate claim delivery to the restaurant, explicit accept before "Accepted", the automatic accepted and ready messages, the exact pickup window with timeline and countdown, delay messages instead of silence, and escalation when the restaurant does not accept — is a proposal from the Nov 8 conversation. Do not build it as locked policy, and do not present it to restaurants or customers as agreed terms, until he agrees it. **Accept deadline TO DECIDE:** how long a restaurant has to accept a claim before the order escalates to the operator (UC-279/UC-281) is not set in §26 — Mahendra must set the number (e.g. in minutes, or by time before the pickup window) before build. If he changes the flow (e.g. accept is per Drop, not per order), §26 must be revised before build. NOTE (V2.7): the 48-hour pickup lead is now LOCKED and is a separate rule from this accept deadline — do not confuse the two; this item stays open only for the accept-deadline number.

18. **Accept deadline and 48-hour lead — PARTLY RESOLVED in V2.7 (Nov 8, 2026, §38 UC-394–UC-403).** (a) Claim-accept deadline: PROPOSED default 2 hours from claim, and no later than 30 minutes before the pickup window opens if the claim arrives late — Mahendra to confirm the number; until he does, UC-279/UC-281 escalation runs on this proposed default in the spec only, not in a build. (b) 48-hour minimum lead from publish to pickup window: **LOCKED Nov 8, 2026 (Mahendra)** — pickup window start must be at least 48 hours after publish; no same-day, no next-day selection. The operator override (explicit, reasoned, audit-logged, UC-396) is part of the locked rule. (c) Offer-confirmation deadline (how long a restaurant has to confirm the exact offer before a scheduled publish passes): TO DECIDE — no number proposed in §38. Open item 17 is NOT closed by §38: it stays open until Mahendra confirms the accept-deadline number.
19. **Customer identity & opt-in recognition — PROPOSED (Nov 8, 2026, §37 UC-382–UC-393).** (a) OTP verification of phone ownership on first claim: PROPOSED, not locked — Mahendra to confirm whether a one-time code is acceptable friction at first checkout, or whether phone entry alone (QR sent to that number) is enough. (b) Returning recognition without re-verification on the same device/browser: PROPOSED — confirm the session/device rule. (c) Ask-once cooldown (how long after a declined opt-in ask before asking again, if ever): TO DECIDE — §37 records "remember the decline, do not nag" with no number. (d) Instagram-only opt-in (a customer whose only channel is Instagram DMs): still open from audit G-37 — §37 covers SMS/WhatsApp/RCS only, because those are the phone channels; Instagram DM opt-in is not specified.
20. **Per-customer cap and reservation hold — PROPOSED, numbers to confirm (Nov 8, 2026, §39 UC-404–UC-409).** Cap: PROPOSED default 3 units per phone per Drop; Mahendra said 3 or 4 — confirm 3 or change to 4. Hold: PROPOSED 10 minutes; confirm the duration. Both are enforced server-side in the spec; neither is locked until he confirms. Note for the record: no cap or hold case existed in V2.5 — Mahendra's recollection that it "was added in the past" does not match the catalog, and §39 is therefore NEW, not a strengthening of an existing case.
21. **Operator alert channels and quiet hours — TO CONFIRM (Nov 8, 2026, §40 UC-410–UC-417).** (a) Alert channel: SMS vs WhatsApp as primary — Mahendra to pick; §40 proposes SMS first with WhatsApp as the channel when the operator prefers it, and SMS fallback if WhatsApp is undelivered. (b) Quiet hours: TO DECIDE — §40 proposes no quiet hours for time-critical pickup/accept alerts during a live window, and no marketing alerts at night, but Mahendra has not set hours. (c) Alert acknowledgement timeout (how long before an unacknowledged alert re-alerts or escalates): TO DECIDE — no number proposed.

22. **Monitoring & auditing numbers — PROPOSED in V2.8 (§42), all TO CONFIRM (Nov 8, 2026).** Mahendra asked for "whatever you think we should have" for monitoring, so these are M2's proposals, not locks: (a) SLOs — 99.9% availability for checkout/pickup during pickup windows, 99.5% for the Studio (UC-435); (b) RTO 4 hours / RPO 1 hour for backup/restore (UC-457); (c) load-test target — 50 concurrent claims and a 15-order simultaneous purchase + simultaneous-scan burst (UC-461); (d) data-retention period per record type (UC-458) — no period proposed, Mahendra/preparer to set; (e) monitoring tool — shortlist Sentry (errors), Vercel Analytics/Logs + Supabase Logs (platform), or a single all-in-one; pick one before Phase 0 (readiness N-10); (f) video-processing provider, E2E tooling — still open from readiness N-09/N-10, unchanged by §42; (g) loss liability — who pays when a refund/dispute exceeds a connected account's balance (UC-446): TO DECIDE, question asked, no answer invented — confirm with Stripe/preparer before the first payout; (h) refund-after-payout rule (UC-444): PROPOSED as deduction from the next settlement — confirm, or pick absorption by DealsPro. Until Mahendra confirms (a)–(h), §42 is a proposal for the numbers and a specification for the mechanisms.
23. **Readiness-audit monitoring items — PARTLY CLOSED by V2.8 (§42).** The following readiness items (pre-build-readiness-audit.md, §3) are now covered as cases: R-01 monitoring/SLO/runbook/status page (UC-435/436), R-10 backup/DR/retention (UC-457/458), R-11 privacy/data-deletion (UC-458/459), R-16 bot/rate-limit/abuse (UC-447, plus UC-433 cap-evasion), R-17 chargeback evidence (UC-445), R-18 refund-after-payout (UC-444, PROPOSED), R-21 video-processing queue/failure (UC-462), R-22 payout failure/retry (UC-443), R-36 outage alert + customer message (UC-439), R-37 webhook failure (UC-438), R-42 consent-list audit/export (UC-459), R-47 deploy/rollback/promotion (UC-440), R-48 load-test target (UC-461, PROPOSED), R-49 monitoring/E2E tooling (UC-435, tool still TO DECIDE — open item 22e). NOT closed by §42 and still open: R-02 accept-deadline numbers, R-03 §25 cancel PROPOSED, R-04 §26 claim→accept PROPOSED, R-05 pickup identity check, R-06 payment-failure flow, R-07 partial cancel, R-13 commission form, R-14 business model, R-15 catalog mismatch (see open item 16 as rewritten), and the remaining P1/P2 readiness items (R-19, R-20, R-23–R-35, R-38–R-41, R-43–R-46, R-50–R-60) — recorded here so they are not mistaken for closed.


24. **Dynamic cuisine filter & hero-dish discovery — PROPOSED in V2.9 (§43, UC-465–UC-478).** (a) The dynamic visibility rule itself (UC-465: show a cuisine chip only when at least one live Drop in that cuisine exists in the selected city; hide it entirely otherwise) is Mahendra's stated rule — confirm it is the final rule before build, including that Coming Soon does not count as live. (b) The cuisine list (UC-466: Indian, Pizza, Thai, Mexican, Chinese, Other) is a proposal — confirm the list, and confirm who may add a cuisine (operator only, proposed). (c) Hero dish (UC-469): one per restaurant, operator-set — confirm the one-per-restaurant limit and whether a restaurant may ask for a hero-dish change without operator approval (proposed: no). (d) "Live" for the filter (UC-476: published + claimable + units remaining + window not ended) — confirm that a paused or cancelled Drop stops counting immediately. (e) Hero-dish tag WORDING (UC-469, V2.12): the PLACEMENT (overlay on top of the hero picture/video, immediately next to the red DROP badge) is Mahendra's decision, Nov 10, 2026 — the wording is not locked: confirm "Hero Dish" as written, or "★ Hero Dish" with a star. Until Mahendra confirms (a)–(e), §43 is a proposal, not a lock.

25. **Restaurant-direct cancel/change — PROPOSED in V2.10 (§25 extension, UC-479–UC-488; revised UC-254–UC-257, UC-261, UC-263, UC-267).** Mahendra's Nov 8, 2026 instruction ("this is very critical") is the model; it is not yet a lock of §25 (open item 15). Confirm before build: (a) the restaurant phone shown to customers is the published/order number, never the owner's personal mobile (UC-485) — confirm which number each restaurant wants shown, and what happens if a restaurant has no published order number; (b) the "no tap, no refund" rule (UC-482) — confirm a verbal cancel with no tap is really no refund, even if the customer produces a call record; (c) the goodwill override after a no-show is the restaurant's tap, once, with a reason (UC-483/UC-261) — confirm the operator has no parallel goodwill override, or state one if he wants it; (d) after Ready, the restaurant cannot self-cancel (UC-484) — confirm the support-case path is operator-decides, and who pays if the operator refunds after Ready. Until Mahendra confirms (a)–(d) and agrees §25, the restaurant-direct model is a proposal, not a lock.

26. **Pickup verification & reminders — PROPOSED in V2.10 (§44, UC-489–UC-502).** Confirm before build: (a) the reminder schedule (UC-492–UC-496: 24h, 2h, window start, midpoint, final 30 min) — confirm the five times, or cut/merge any of them; (b) the window-end unclaimed rule (UC-497) vs the 30-minute grace + operator-decision rule (grace wording retained in open item 29d; formerly UC-307/UC-308 — UC-307 deleted in V2.17) — pick ONE: adopt the V2.10 window-end marking (and revise UC-308/UC-261 accordingly) or keep grace + operator decision; do not build both; (c) the pickup identity check (UC-500, readiness R-05) — pick a check (name match, last-4-digits of phone, OTP at pickup) or explicitly accept no identity check for the first Drop; (d) the goodwill-refund decision-maker after a no-show — restaurant tap (UC-483/UC-497) or operator override (UC-261) — confirm one, or state both with a stated order. Until Mahendra confirms (a)–(d), §44 is a proposal, not a lock.

**Parallel-work conflict, recorded in this item:** a §45 "Restaurant order management" framing (UC-489–UC-502, restaurant no-login order view) was written into this file by another agent in the same hour as §44, including its own open item and traceability note, but no §45 cases were ever added to the catalog body — the IDs UC-489–UC-502 are the §44 cases above. The §45 proposal's own open questions are preserved here so they are not lost, as framing only, not as cases: (i) customer-phone visibility window (from claim until 24 hours after the pickup window ends, then masked again) — confirm 24 hours, or pick a shorter/longer window; (ii) offline queue-and-sync behaviour — PROPOSED, not a proven capability; prove in staging (including two taps on the same order while offline) before promising it to a restaurant; (iii) per-staff access: one shared counter link per location in the first release, or per-staff links — confirm, and if per-staff, who issues and revokes them; (iv) the UC-308 refinement (restaurant goodwill tap is the decision; operator decision remains only when the restaurant does not act or a second refund is requested) — confirm the operator has no standing parallel goodwill override. If the §45 restaurant order-management view is wanted, it needs its own new IDs after UC-502, written as cases — do not renumber §44 to make room for it.


27. **Top 5 in your area this week + interactive matcher — PROPOSED in V2.11 (§46, UC-516–UC-529).** Confirm before build: (a) ranking weights (UC-517: PROPOSED 40% sellout velocity / 30% pickup reliability / 20% repeat-buyer rate / 10% recency) — confirm the weights, and confirm whether pickup reliability should count at all before a Drop has any pickups yet (a new Drop has no pickup history); (b) recompute latency (UC-518: PROPOSED under 60 seconds from event to new order) — confirm or pick a different target; (c) "Selling fast" threshold (UC-528: PROPOSED 50% of units claimed within the first 24 hours live) — confirm the threshold, and confirm whether any other customer-facing badge is wanted; (d) Top 5 vs Top 3 on mobile (UC-516/UC-529: Mahendra said "top 3 or 5" — confirm 5 total with 3 visible first on mobile, or 3 total); (e) matcher question wording (UC-522: confirm the exact Q1/Q2 wording and chip labels, including "Surprise me" and the pickup-day options Tonight / Friday vs a date picker). Until Mahendra confirms (a)–(e), §46 is a proposal, not a lock.

28. **Food-ready notifications & customer check-in — PROPOSED in V2.13 (§47, UC-530–UC-547).** Confirm before build: (a) channel order (UC-535: SMS first via Twilio + A2P 10DLC, WhatsApp utility template as fallback — confirm SMS-first, or pick WhatsApp-first; WhatsApp US utility pricing was unverified at research time, re-check before budgeting it as cheaper); (b) message cap (UC-544: PROPOSED max 2 messages per order per Drop — confirmation + ready — plus at most 1 reminder) — confirm the cap; (c) quiet hours (UC-546: PROPOSED no marketing 9:00 PM–9:00 AM local, stricter 8am–8pm in FL/WA/MD/OK — confirm the hours with counsel/provider, and confirm the transactional exemption for live-window ready alerts); (d) batch-confirm wording (UC-532/UC-537: PROPOSED 'Send ready to N customers?' + 'Is the food bagged?' — confirm the wording); (e) check-in auto-reply (UC-542: PROPOSED optional 'We're bagging yours now — come to the counter' — confirm on by default or off by default); (f) §45 dependency — UC-509 (Mark Ready) lives in the unmerged §45 draft; confirm §45 merges first, or UC-509 is specified as a case before UC-530/UC-531 are built. Until Mahendra confirms (a)–(f), §47 is a proposal, not a lock.

29. **Five-step pickup timeline — PROPOSED in V2.14 (§48, UC-548–UC-557).** Confirm before build: (a) the five states as the canonical model (UC-548: Claimed → Accepted → Preparing → Ready → Picked Up, no state skipped, no timer-only transition) — confirm, or cut/merge any state; (b) the customer timeline wording (UC-549: "They've got your order — no need to come early" at Accepted, Ready-by estimate that moves only on a Running-late tap) — confirm the wording; (c) the QR/code handover (UC-552/UC-553: single-use QR, 3-character code fallback, staff-only rack, no open shelf) — confirm the code length and the no-open-shelf rule; (d) the unclaimed rule — PROPOSED 30-minute grace after window end, then Unclaimed, no automatic refund (wording retained here in this item; formerly UC-554, DELETED in V2.17 as a duplicate statement) vs UC-497 (§44: Unclaimed at window end, no grace) vs the 30-minute grace + operator-decision rule (§29, formerly UC-307/UC-308 — UC-307 deleted in V2.17) — pick ONE rule; do not build both rules. **V2.19 note:** §51 (UC-577) proposes a 15-minute grace as the single rule — if adopted, it settles this item and open item 26(b), and revises UC-497; (e) the scorecard targets (UC-557: PROPOSED ≥95% Ready on time, median counter wait <60s / p90 <3min, QR first-scan ≥95%, no-show <3%, wrong-person 0, order accuracy ≥99%) — confirm the targets, or pick different ones to validate in the first 3–5 Drops. Until Mahendra confirms (a)–(e), §48 is a proposal, not a lock.

30. **DealsPro MCP server — PROPOSED in V2.15 (§49, UC-558–UC-567).** Confirm before build: (a) MCP open to all assistants (agreement 0.2) — the whole §49 assumes the open door; if the door stays closed, §49 is re-scoped; (b) the 7-tool catalog (UC-558: search_drops, get_drop, claim_drop, get_order, cancel_order, get_pickup_status, list_restaurants) — confirm the list, or add/remove tools; (c) run_code as candidate 8th tool (Code Mode) — confirm it as a tool or keep the door at 7; a script runs in a sandbox with no open internet, talks only to the MCP server, uses the caller's existing permissions, is audit-logged, and returns only a short summary — it must never publish a Drop, move money, or bypass the two human gates; (d) the identity handshake (UC-563: checkout phone is identity, E.164-normalised) — the handshake mechanism itself (per-user OAuth2 with attribution is the candidate) is NOT settled and needs a separate design decision; (e) deferred loading (UC-559) — confirm the just-in-time tool-search step. Until Mahendra confirms (a)–(e), §49 is a proposal, not a lock.

31. **Mobile light loading — claim first — LOCKED Nov 22, 2026 (Mahendra: "Lock it as-is").** (a) The 2-second budget is LOCKED as proposed — claim interactive within 2 seconds on a throttled 4G connection in staging. (b) Load order vs visual order is LOCKED as written — the claim is usable before the video finishes loading, while the video stays the visual hero (UC-228). (c) Scope is LOCKED as written — the customer Drop page only. §50 is a lock, not a proposal.

32. **Drop timing — PROPOSED in V2.19 (§51, UC-569–UC-584).** Confirm before build: (a) Option C as the first-Drop schedule (UC-569) — confirm the Mon 2 PM publish / Wed 3 PM cut-off / 6–8 PM pickup shape, or pick different clock times; (b) the 15-minute grace (UC-577) as the single unclaimed rule — adopt it (settling open items 26b and 29d and revising UC-497) or pick one of the existing three statements instead; (c) the SMS cadence (UC-581) against UC-250's 1/week marketing cap (open item 14d) — confirm 3 texts per Drop or shrink to announcement + final call; (d) the food-safety holding rule (UC-580) with the local health department — confirm Dallas/Frisco/Prosper adoption of the FDA Food Code and the restaurant's holding practice; (e) the 3-hour prep window (UC-584) in the first restaurant's own kitchen — confirm vessel/burner capacity, or move the cut-off to 2 PM / pickup to 7 PM. Until Mahendra confirms (a)–(e), §51 is a proposal, not a lock.

33. **Location gate — PROPOSED in V2.20 (§52, UC-585–UC-594).** The gate itself was DECIDED Nov 22, 2026 (Mahendra: a confirmed location is required before claiming); the cases are the written form of that decision, all PROPOSED until confirmed as written. Confirm before build: (a) the gate placement (UC-585) — at claim, before the claim is created, browsing never gated; (b) the method combination (UC-586) — confirm all five methods, or drop one; (c) the mismatch rule (UC-589) — warn with distance + twice-confirmed "claim anyway", never block; (d) the TDPSA consent and privacy-notice wording (UC-593) — confirm with counsel, including the small-business exemption question; (e) the first-use flow (UC-592) — confirm the guess → tap → picker → save order, and that the resolved city (not raw coordinates) is what gets stored. Until Mahendra confirms (a)–(e), §52 is a proposal, not a lock.

34. **Differentiation — PROPOSED in V2.20 (§53, UC-595–UC-606).** Confirm before build: (a) the lead (UC-595) — verifiable dollar saving + portion guarantee on every Drop; (b) the Top-5-only discovery rule (UC-597) — confirm no browse-everything catalogue, ever; (c) the SMS-first claim (UC-598) — confirm no app and no account at claim; (d) the two do-not-copy rules (UC-604 Groupon, UC-605 DoorDash lock-in) — confirm both as standing rules; (e) the bundle sweet spot (UC-606) — confirm it stays undecided and is set from the first Drops' repeat-purchase evidence, not from a practitioner percentage. Until Mahendra confirms (a)–(e), §53 is a proposal, not a lock.

35. **Customer preview — PROPOSED in V2.21 (§56, UC-613–UC-622).** Confirm before build: (a) the greeting (UC-613) — time-of-day + name from the phone identity, no name shown when unknown, never "Guest"; (b) the dish sub-chip list (UC-614) — confirm the nine groups and that the list is operator-set; (c) the 16-city list (UC-615) — confirm the sixteen cities; (d) the 4:30 PM alert (UC-617) — confirm the 4:30 PM CT time, one text per day at most, and how it fits the UC-250 weekly marketing cap (open item 14) — the daily cadence is proposed, the cap governs; (e) the Indian-only bundle view (UC-621) — confirm bundles only, Indian only, no solo meals, as the launch frame. Until Mahendra confirms (a)–(e), §56 is a proposal, not a lock.

\* UC-016 kept in substance (genuine verified regular price); its note now ties to the serves/price verification in the review checklist.
**Outdated / contradicting a newer decision (fixed in V2.0):** (a) any "Onboarding" stage wording — renamed "Getting ready", still pipeline; (b) any implication that a pipeline lead is "doing business" before its first Drop goes live; (c) any auto-advance of pipeline stages — now manual + confirm; (d) the V1.0 assumption that restaurants cannot be removed — now removable with history kept; (e) distribution channel lists that included email — email is NOT a channel; (f) the duplicated UC-121–UC-124 IDs; (g) the Oct 30 preservation rule as the build approach — superseded by the Nov 8 full-rebuild decision (RESOLVED in V2.1, open item 1).
**Outdated / contradicting a newer decision (fixed in V2.1):** (h) the smart-menu target of 2–3 Drops per restaurant — now 3 to 4, 3 sweet spot, 4 max, never a filler Drop; (i) any long / UUID-based URL in a generated link — every generated URL must be tiny (UC-233); (j) any implication that video is optional — video is a MUST for every Drop; (k) any password-based Studio login — login is by magic link (Supabase Auth); (l) any plan for a native app now — no native app yet, mobile-first web only (UC-243).
**Outdated / contradicting a newer decision (fixed in V2.2):** (m) any assumption that creator or restaurant payouts are manual / ad-hoc Zelle at scale — payouts run through Stripe Connect (UC-245, UC-246, UC-253); Money *calculating* a share is not paying it.
**Missing in V1.0 (added in V2.0):** city hierarchy; Doing Business vs Pipeline split; pipeline stages/confirm/manual moves; Getting-ready rule; restaurant removal; private create links; commission field; Bundle/Non-bundle serves; Demand view; Money top-level; coverage/escalation/roles; mobile-first layout; creator-distribution optionality.

# The catalog

Status tags: **KEPT** = unchanged from V1.0. **REVISED** = content changed; change + reason noted. **REN** = renumbered only (old ID in brackets). **NEW** = added in V2.0.

## §1 Restaurant onboarding & management — P0

- **UC-001** View all restaurants in Studio (P0) — KEPT.
- **UC-002** Search restaurants by name (P0) — KEPT.
- **UC-003** Create a new restaurant profile (P0) — **REVISED.** Location is entered ONLY via cascading State → Metroplex → Sub-city picklists (UC-195); no free-text city field anywhere. Reason: city hierarchy lock, Nov 8.
- **UC-004** Edit restaurant name, address, and contact information (P0) — **REVISED.** Same picklist rule as UC-003; editing the city re-cascades metroplex/sub-city. Reason: city hierarchy lock.
- **UC-005** Configure pickup location and customer instructions (P0) — KEPT.
- **UC-006** Configure restaurant timezone (P0) — KEPT.
- **UC-007** Activate or deactivate a restaurant (P0) — **REVISED.** Deactivate is a status tag only — the restaurant stays listed. Full removal is UC-200 (Remove from Studio), not UC-007. Reason: removal rules added Nov 8; operators confused deactivate with delete.
- **UC-008** Select a restaurant while creating a Drop (P0) — KEPT.
- **UC-009** Open an individual restaurant workspace (P0) — **REVISED.** Workspace shows, in one place: active Drops, drafts, media, orders, performance, agreed commission, private Drop-create link, and city/metroplex/sub-city. Reason: Restaurants-section lock (Oct 30) + commission + private link, Nov 7.
- **UC-010** View all Drops belonging to a restaurant (P0) — KEPT.
- **UC-195** Pick location via State → Metroplex → Sub-city picklists, with counts per sub-city including 0-count sub-cities and an honest empty state (P0) — NEW. Default Texas › Dallas–Fort Worth Metroplex. Every city label carries its state ("Prosper, TX"). Breadcrumb reads State › Metroplex › Sub-city.
- **UC-196** View restaurants by city as Doing Business vs Pipeline, scoped by the location picklist (P0) — NEW. Doing Business = live restaurants; Pipeline = everyone else. Counts per city/metroplex/state update with the picklist.
- **UC-197** Move a pipeline lead through stages To pitch → Pitched → Talking → Getting ready, plus Paused parked outside the line, by tapping the EXACT target stage and confirming "Move X from [old] to [new]?" (P0) — NEW. No auto-advance, no "Advance" button; any stage may be jumped to forward/back/Paused; tapping alone changes nothing; change is audit-logged with a toast. "Log touch" updates last touch only and never changes stage.
- **UC-198** "Getting ready" is still pipeline, NOT doing business; a restaurant moves to Doing Business only when its first Drop goes live (P0) — NEW. Getting ready is styled "in process" (dashed amber), never green/live, with a banner "Still in pipeline — no live Drop, no sales". The move to Doing Business is an explicit, operator-confirmed transition — never silent (see open item 6).
- **UC-199** Remove a pipeline lead from the pipeline with confirm + Undo (P1) — NEW. Allowed for any lead (leads have no Drops, orders, or sales); confirm spells out the removal; deletion is audit-logged; Undo restores it.
- **UC-200** Remove a Doing Business restaurant from the Studio: hide it from Doing Business and city lists, revoke its private Drop-create link, keep Drops/orders/Money records reachable, list it in "Removed — history kept" with Restore (P0) — NEW. Restore returns it to Doing Business but the create link stays revoked until regenerated. Never cascade-delete history.
- **UC-201** Permanently delete a restaurant ONLY when it has 0 Drops, 0 orders, and 0 media (P0) — NEW. Mirrors the UC-059 Drop-delete rule. Any other restaurant gets UC-200 (hide with history), never permanent delete.
- **UC-202** Show per-lead last touch, next step, owner + backup on every pipeline card (P1) — NEW. No lead sits without a next step or an owner; the owner is never just Mahendra (see §21).
- **UC-203** Give each restaurant a unique private Drop-create link (`dealspro.ai/r/[slug]/create?key=…`), no login (P0) — NEW. Share only with that restaurant. Submissions land in Review & Queue marked Restaurant-created with the restaurant named, and can never publish directly — operator approval still required.
- **UC-204** Regenerate or revoke a restaurant's private create link; regenerating kills the old link immediately; revoking is operator-only and audit-logged (P0) — NEW. A removed restaurant's link is revoked automatically (UC-200).

## §2 Drop creation & offer details — P0

- **UC-011** Create a new Drop under a restaurant (P0) — KEPT.
- **UC-012** Select or enter a dish name (P0) — KEPT.
- **UC-013** Enter dish description and exact portion (P0) — **REVISED.** Portion now includes the required Bundle vs Non-bundle choice and serves size (UC-205); a portion/serves change after restaurant confirmation voids confirmation (same as price/quantity/window changes). Reason: serves lock, Nov 7.
- **UC-014** Select the dish's photo or video (P0) — KEPT.
- **UC-015** Set the Drop selling price (P0) — KEPT.
- **UC-016** Enter a genuine, verified regular menu price (P0) — KEPT (note: verified price is checked in review, UC-065; never invent a compare-at price).
- **UC-017** Set the maximum quantity available (P0) — KEPT.
- **UC-018** Configure pickup date and time window (P0) — KEPT (structured dates/times/timezone/cutoff, not free text — prototype flaw, must not carry).
- **UC-019** Configure the order cutoff time (P0) — KEPT.
- **UC-020** Save an unpublished draft (P0) — KEPT.
- **UC-205** Require a Bundle vs Non-bundle choice at Drop creation, with serves: Non-bundle = Serves 1 only; Bundle = Serves 2, 3, 4, 5, or 6+ (P0) — NEW. A Drop cannot be created without it. Switching type swaps the serves options; Serves 1 never appears on a Bundle and 2+ never on a Non-bundle.
- **UC-206** Show serving size on the Drop — on the card, in review, and on the customer page — as "Bundle · Serves 4" / "Bundle · Serves 6+" / "Non-bundle · Serves 1", including a Serves pill on the hero photo/video (P1) — NEW. Reason: serves-visibility lock, Nov 7 (on-picture pill; top-right BUNDLE badge was tried and removed — it looked bad — do not re-add it).

## §3 Video-first preview & publication — P0

- **UC-021** Upload an actual-food video (P0) — **REVISED in V2.1.** Video is a MUST for every Drop — no Drop without a real video hero (9:16, muted loop, poster fallback). A still photo alone is not a Drop. Reason: video-is-a-must lock, Nov 8.
- **UC-022** Upload a thumbnail or fallback photo (P0) — KEPT.
- **UC-023** Associate media with the correct restaurant and dish (P0) — KEPT.
- **UC-024** Preview the customer experience in vertical 9:16 format (P0) — KEPT.
- **UC-025** Preview muted autoplay and fallback behavior (P0) — KEPT.
- **UC-026** Preview price, quantity, pickup details, and Claim button (P0) — KEPT.
- **UC-027** Validate all required fields before publishing (P0) — KEPT.
- **UC-028** Record restaurant confirmation of the offer (P0) — KEPT.
- **UC-029** Record operator approval and publish the approved offer (P0) — KEPT.
- **UC-030** Copy or open the published Drop URL (P0) — **REVISED in V2.1.** The published Drop URL is a TINY URL with a short server-generated code (UC-233) — e.g. `dealspro.ai/d/abc123` — never a long slug or UUID. Reason: tiny-URL rule, Nov 8.
- Locked rule (applies to §3): preview must replicate EXACTLY the live Drop page — one shared component, sharing Drop state so a claim in one place moves counts everywhere; sold-out/ended Drops preview as unavailable. Video is a reusable asset attached to the restaurant/menu item, NOT to a single Drop; no price, date, or Claim text burned into the video — CTA stays an HTML overlay; tapping the video or the sticky Claim bar opens the claim sheet; sound/pause are small corner controls, no dead taps.

## §4 Payment, QR & first redemption — P0

- **UC-031** Display actual Live status (P0) — KEPT.
- **UC-032** Display sold and remaining quantities (P0) — KEPT.
- **UC-033** Enforce maximum inventory (P0) — KEPT.
- **UC-034** Open the existing customer checkout flow (P0) — KEPT.

- **UC-036** Generate the customer's QR ticket (P0) — **REVISED in V2.1.** The QR encodes a TINY URL (UC-233) with a short, unique, unguessable code per order — e.g. `dealspro.ai/q/abc123` — generated ON DEMAND when the order is paid, not pre-generated in bulk. Reason: tiny-URL rule + QR scaling, Nov 8.
- **UC-037** Display paid orders in Studio (P0) — KEPT.
- **UC-038** Scan and redeem each QR ticket exactly once (P0) — **REVISED in V2.1.** Redemption is ATOMIC in Supabase: one scan checks the code, marks it redeemed, and cannot be reused, even under simultaneous scans. Reason: QR scaling rule — scaling lives in the database/redemption check, not the QR image, Nov 8.
- **UC-039** Show redeemed or picked-up status (P0) — KEPT.
- **UC-040** End or cancel a Drop without deleting paid-order history (P0) — KEPT.

## §12 Distribution, attribution & outcomes — P1

- **UC-111** Copy the standard Drop URL (P1) — **REVISED in V2.1.** The standard Drop URL is the TINY URL (UC-233). Reason: tiny-URL rule, Nov 8.
- **UC-112** Generate URLs using the existing ?tag= attribution mechanism (P1) — **REVISED in V2.1.** One tag parameter with SHORT tag values (`?tag=whatsapp`, `?tag=insta`, …) so attribution does not defeat the tiny-URL rule; do not add a second `?ref=`. Reason: tiny-URL rule, Nov 8.
- **UC-113** Prepare WhatsApp sharing copy (P1) — **REVISED.** Channel set is locked: WhatsApp, Instagram, Facebook, SMS alert, RCS, TikTok, QR. NO email channel anywhere. Reason: distribution-channels lock, Nov 7.
- **UC-114** Prepare Instagram sharing copy (P1) — **REVISED.** Same channel-set lock as UC-113; Instagram in-app browser blocks Apple Pay — nudge to Safari/real browser.
- **UC-115** Prepare Facebook sharing copy (P1) — **REVISED.** Same channel-set lock as UC-113.
- **UC-116** Prepare SMS sharing copy (P1) — **REVISED.** Same channel-set lock as UC-113; SMS alert is a distribution channel, distinct from transactional QR texts.
- **UC-117** Prepare RCS sharing copy (P1) — KEPT.
- **UC-118** Prepare TikTok sharing copy (P1) — KEPT.
- **UC-119** Generate the QR code and creator link for the Drop (P1) — **REVISED.** The creator link exists ONLY when the per-Drop distribution choice is not Owned only (UC-209). Reason: creator-distribution optionality, Nov 7.
- **UC-120** Manage distribution activities separately from publication (P1) — KEPT.
- **UC-121** Track visits and checkout events where instrumentation supports them (P1) — KEPT.
- **UC-122** Attribute paid, refunded, and redeemed orders to channels (P1) — KEPT.
- **UC-123** Display units sold, sales value, and redemption counts per Drop (P1) — KEPT.
- **UC-124** Compare restaurant and Drop performance to identify repeat opportunities (P1) — KEPT.
- **UC-209** Choose distribution per Drop: Owned only vs Selected creators vs Area pick list; Owned only shows no creator link and no creator commission (P1) — NEW. Distribution is OPTIONAL per Drop, not mandatory.
- **UC-210** No email as a distribution channel, anywhere in the Studio (P0) — NEW. Rule, not a feature: email must not appear in the composer picker, channel links, or results.
- **UC-211** One permanent creator link per creator (parent), with per-Drop tagged links auto-generated underneath; creators pick approved Drops in their Dallas area, capped per Drop, paid on pickup only (P1) — NEW, PROPOSAL ONLY, not locked. Manual parent/child link management was judged too complex to scale; the simplified one-link version is a proposal. Creator share is a separate line from restaurant commission. (See open item 4.)

## §13 Financial operations & reconciliation — P1 (renumbered from old UC-121–UC-130)

- **UC-125** [old UC-121] Display gross sales by restaurant and period (P1) — REN.
- **UC-126** [old UC-122] Display available Stripe processing-fee information (P1) — REN.
- **UC-127** [old UC-123] Calculate estimated DealsPro revenue (P1) — REN.
- **UC-128** [old UC-124] Calculate restaurant settlement amounts under agreed terms (P1) — REN.
- **UC-129** [old UC-125] Track payment and payout statuses independently (P1) — REN.
- **UC-130** [old UC-126] Identify payments needing reconciliation (P1) — REN.
- **UC-131** [old UC-127] Record refunds and refund reasons (P1) — REN.
- **UC-132** [old UC-128] Track disputes and chargebacks (P1) — REN.
- **UC-133** [old UC-129] Export reconciled restaurant statements (P1) — REN.

- Settlement formula (locked): Net owed = gross paid − refunds − est. Stripe fees − restaurant commission − creator share. Commission and creator share count ONLY on redeemed/picked-up orders; $0 on visits, clicks, or paid-but-not-picked-up orders.
- **UC-207** Record the agreed commission on the restaurant record: % per redeemed order + agreed-on date + agreed with / recorded by; show it on the restaurant card, workspace, Drop review, and Money (P0) — NEW. Displayed rate is SAMPLE / EXAMPLE ONLY until a real rate is agreed. Form (% vs flat vs monthly, per-restaurant vs per-Drop) is UNDECIDED — see open item 3.
- **UC-208** Changing a restaurant's commission after it has confirmed a Drop flags every confirmed Drop for that restaurant for re-confirmation, same as a price change; it will not publish until the restaurant confirms again; the change is audit-logged (P0) — NEW. **EXPANDED in V2.8 (Studio UC-421, mismatch fix):** A material change to a confirmed Drop (price, quantity, pickup window, video, or dish/portion) AUTO-VOIDS the prior confirmation — the Drop is recorded as voided, not merely flagged, and cannot publish until the restaurant confirms the new version. This is the same re-confirmation rule as a commission change: the confirmation belongs to the offer version it confirmed, and a changed offer has no confirmation.

## §15 Operational alerts & automation — P1 (renumbered from old UC-141–UC-150)

- **UC-145** [old UC-141] Alert operators when approval is required (P1) — **REN + REVISED.** Alerts now go to the primary AND, after the 2-hour wait, to the backup approver (UC-220); the alert shows the owner and waiting clock. Reason: no-single-point-of-failure, Nov 7. **EXPANDED in V2.8 (Studio UC-426, mismatch fix):** Every alert rule names its owner — operator or backup (UC-220/UC-221) — so an alert with no owner is a broken rule, not an unassigned alert.
- **UC-146** [old UC-142] Alert when publishing fails (P1) — REN.
- **UC-147** [old UC-143] Alert when inventory is nearly exhausted (P1) — REN.
- **UC-148** [old UC-144] Detect inconsistent payment and order states (P1) — REN.
- **UC-149** [old UC-145] Alert before upcoming pickup windows (P1) — REN.
- **UC-150** [old UC-146] Detect repeated redemption failures (P1) — REN.
- **UC-151** [old UC-147] Alert when published media becomes unavailable (P1) — REN.
- **UC-152** [old UC-148] Identify customer orders requiring assistance (P1) — REN.
- **UC-153** [old UC-149] Generate an end-of-day operations summary (P1) — REN.
- **UC-154** [old UC-150] Flag restaurants with repeated fulfillment problems (P1) — REN.

- **UC-155** [old UC-151] Authenticate Studio operators (P0, Preview) — **REN + REVISED in V2.1.** Studio login is by MAGIC LINK (Supabase Auth): operator enters their email, clicks the link, and is in — no passwords, no password-reset flow. Roles are now defined (UC-223): Operator, Backup operator, Restaurant manager, Viewer. Reason: magic-link lock, Nov 8; Team & Coverage lock, Nov 7.

Reason: Team & Coverage lock.
- **UC-157** [old UC-153] Restrict restaurant-specific users to authorized records (P0) — REN.
- **UC-158** [old UC-154] Persist administrative create, edit, approval, publish, and deletion events (P0) — REN.
- **UC-159** [old UC-155] Display who changed a Drop and what was changed (P0) — REN.

- **UC-163** [old UC-159] Expose actionable integration failures without leaking sensitive data (P0) — REN.
- **UC-164** [old UC-160] Support recovery from failed operations and jobs (P0) — REN.

## §17 LLM Drops & smart menus — P1 (renumbered from old UC-161–UC-175)

- **UC-165** [old UC-161] Create Drops using conversational AI instructions (P1) — REN.
- **UC-166** [old UC-162] Generate a draft using approved restaurant and menu information (P1) — REN.
- **UC-167** [old UC-163] Generate dish descriptions and customer-facing offer copy (P1) — REN.
- **UC-168** [old UC-164] Match the exact dish with an approved video (P1) — REN.
- **UC-169** [old UC-165] Use previous Drops as context for creating new drafts (P1) — REN.
- **UC-170** [old UC-166] Detect missing, ambiguous, or contradictory offer details (P1) — REN.
- **UC-171** [old UC-167] Require human confirmation of price, quantity, and pickup details (P1) — REN.
- **UC-172** [old UC-168] Generate WhatsApp, Instagram, Facebook, SMS, RCS, and TikTok promotional copy (P1) — REN.

- **UC-174** [old UC-170] Display a small curated smart menu, with 3 to 4 active Drops per restaurant (P1) — **REN + REVISED in V2.1.** The smart menu is a page for each restaurant with **3 to 4 Drops**, called the smart menu. 3 is the sweet spot, 4 is the max, NEVER a filler Drop — a weak Drop added only to fill the page is forbidden. The page works at 1, 2, 3, or 4 Drops. SUPERSEDES the old 2–3 target (V2.0 open item 5, resolved Nov 8).
- **UC-175** [old UC-171] Use the restaurant's tiny smart-menu URL to route customers based on claimable Drops (P1) — **REN + REVISED in V2.1.** The smart-menu URL is a TINY URL, unique to the restaurant's LOCATION (UC-235) — not the old long `/r/[slug]` form. 1 claimable Drop → feature it; 2–4 → compact video-friendly smart menu; 0 → UC-236 fallback. Reason: tiny-URL rule + smart-menu lock, Nov 8.
- **UC-176** [old UC-172] Keep sold-out and ended offers visible as unavailable where appropriate (P1) — **REN + REVISED in V2.1.** A sold-out Drop stays VISIBLE on the smart menu until its pickup window ends, marked "Sold out" with NO buy button (it proves demand); after the window ends it drops off the smart menu. Ended Drops are never purchasable. Reason: smart-menu edge-case rule, Nov 8.
- **UC-177** [old UC-173] Recommend previous dishes worth repeating (P1) — REN.
- **UC-178** [old UC-174] Maintain restaurant-specific dish and video context for AI (P1) — REN.
- **UC-179** [old UC-175] Clearly distinguish AI-generated drafts from approved and published offers (P1) — REN.
- **UC-234** Show one smart-menu page per restaurant: the restaurant's live Drops (3–4 target), each with video hero (with the UC-469 Hero Dish tag overlaid on top of the picture, immediately next to the red DROP badge, on a hero-dish Drop — REVISED in V2.12), price, real scarcity, and its own pickup window (P0) — NEW in V2.1. This is the restaurant's page on DealsPro — not a list of every Drop ever, just what is live now.
- **UC-235** Give each partner restaurant TWO tiny URLs, unique to its location: (1) the smart-menu URL to promote to users, (2) the private Drop-create URL to add Drops with Mahendra's approval (P0) — NEW in V2.1. One location = two tiny links: one to sell, one to create. A chain's locations each get their own pair; inventory and pickup are per location, never shared across locations.
- **UC-236** When a restaurant has 0 live Drops, its smart menu NEVER shows an empty page: show "Next Drop coming soon" with the restaurant, a photo, and an alert signup (SMS / WhatsApp, unchecked by default) (P0) — NEW in V2.1. The tiny URL must always lead somewhere useful, even between Drops.
- **UC-237** A smart menu with 1–2 Drops is fine — never add a filler Drop to reach 3–4 (P1) — NEW in V2.1. A sold-out 2-Drop menu beats a 4-Drop menu with one Drop nobody wanted.
- **UC-238** Show each Drop's pickup window unmissably on the smart menu — date + time in big type on that Drop's card, never buried in details (P0) — NEW in V2.1. If two Drops share a window, say so; if they do not, make the difference obvious. A customer must never guess which window belongs to which Drop.
- **UC-239** Creator credit from a smart-menu link: if a creator shares the smart-menu URL and a customer buys ANY Drop from that visit, that is the creator's sale — the tag travels with the visit, not only with a single Drop link (P1) — NEW in V2.1; attribution rule **LOCKED in V2.7 (Mahendra, Nov 8, 2026): last-tagged-link-wins (UC-248)** — the smart-menu tag counts as a tag, so it earns the sale unless the customer taps a different creator's tagged link before purchase, in which case the last tag wins. Paid on pickup, same as always (UC-211). If this rule were not in place, creators would only share single Drops and the smart menu would never get promoted.
- **UC-240** Provide a restaurant promotion kit for the smart menu: a QR poster for the counter, a one-line WhatsApp message the restaurant can forward, and a bio link (P1) — NEW in V2.1. A tiny URL that sits in a drawer is useless; the kit is what gets the smart menu promoted.
- **UC-241** Reuse one video per dish across Drops: a new Drop of the same dish/presentation reuses the approved video; a new video is required only for a new dish or a changed portion/presentation (P0) — NEW in V2.1. This is the only way 3–4 Drops per restaurant is operable, given video is a must (UC-021).
- **UC-242** Smart-menu video rule: every Drop on the smart menu has its own video hero (reused per UC-241 where the dish is the same); no Drop appears on the smart menu with a still photo only (P0) — NEW in V2.1.
- Agent-system note (design only, endorsed Nov 2): one orchestrator owns the Drop state through the 8 steps (Restaurant → Draft → Video + Smart Menu → Approval → Publish/Schedule → Sell → Fulfill → Resolve). Specialists return evidence-backed proposals on that shared Drop record; they never change inventory, price, or publish status; disagreements block publishing and surface to the operator. North-star metrics: redeemed purchases and repeat buyers. This is NOT a build authorization.

- **UC-181** [old UC-177] Cancel a paid, unredeemed order before pickup (P0) — REN.
- **UC-182** [old UC-178] Issue a full refund through Stripe (P0) — REN.
- **UC-183** [old UC-179] Support authorized partial refunds (P0) — REN.
- **UC-184** [old UC-180] Require a cancellation reason (P0) — REN.
- **UC-185** [old UC-181] Prevent ordinary cancellation of redeemed orders; require an exception process (P0) — REN.

- **UC-191** [old UC-187] Include cancellations and refunds in restaurant financial reports (P0) — REN.
